#ifdef VERTEX
#version 300 es

#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform
#endif
#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x) layout(location = x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(binding = x, std140)
#else
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(std140)
#endif
uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
vec4 u_xlat0;
bool u_xlatb0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
mediump float u_xlat16_2;
float u_xlat9;
float u_xlat10;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat16_2 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat0.x = (-u_xlat16_2) + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat0.w = u_xlat0.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.w = clamp(u_xlat0.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    u_xlat10 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat10 = inversesqrt(u_xlat10);
    u_xlat0.xyz = vec3(u_xlat10) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1 = u_xlat0;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat1.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat1.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat1.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat9 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat9 = inversesqrt(u_xlat9);
    u_xlat1.xyz = vec3(u_xlat9) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat16_2 = u_xlat16_2 + u_xlat16_2;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw = u_xlat1.xyz * (-vec3(u_xlat16_2)) + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, (-u_xlat0.xyz));
    u_xlat16_2 = clamp(u_xlat16_2, 0.0, 1.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2 = (-u_xlat16_2) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2 = u_xlat16_2 * u_xlat16_2;
    vs_TEXCOORD5.w = u_xlat16_2 * u_xlat16_2;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.x = 0.0;
    return;
}

#endif
#ifdef FRAGMENT
#version 300 es
#ifdef GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod
#extension GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod : enable
#endif

precision highp float;
precision highp int;
#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform
#endif
#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x) layout(location = x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(binding = x, std140)
#else
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(std140)
#endif
uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform highp sampler2D unity_NHxRoughness;
UNITY_LOCATION(3) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
vec3 u_xlat6;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
mediump float u_xlat16_20;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.xz = (-vec2(_Glossiness)) + vec2(1.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_1.x = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_1.x;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat16_1.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_1 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, u_xlat16_1.x);
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_1.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_2.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx;
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_20 = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_20) * u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_20 = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_6.x = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat0.y = u_xlat16_6.x;
    u_xlat0.x = texture(unity_NHxRoughness, u_xlat0.yz).x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * 16.0;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat6.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_3.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    u_xlat16_20 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat6.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_20) + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = (-u_xlat16_3.xyz) + vs_TEXCOORD1.www;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD5.www * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat16_3.xyz + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

#endif


#ifdef VERTEX
#version 300 es

#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform
#endif
#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x) layout(location = x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(binding = x, std140)
#else
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(std140)
#endif
uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD7;
out highp vec3 vs_TEXCOORD8;
vec4 u_xlat0;
bool u_xlatb0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
float u_xlat6;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat0.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD3 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat0.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat0.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat6 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat6 = inversesqrt(u_xlat6);
    vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz = vec3(u_xlat6) * u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD5 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD7 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    return;
}

#ifdef FRAGMENT
#version 300 es
#ifdef GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod
#extension GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod : enable
#endif

precision highp float;
precision highp int;
#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform
#endif
#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x) layout(location = x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(binding = x, std140)
#else
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(std140)
#endif
uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_1;
vec3 u_xlat2;
vec3 u_xlat3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
vec3 u_xlat5;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_5;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
vec3 u_xlat7;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_8;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_11;
float u_xlat14;
float u_xlat21;
mediump float u_xlat16_22;
void main()
{
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_1.x = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_1.x;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat7.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz) * u_xlat0.xxx + _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat3.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_8.x = dot(u_xlat7.xyz, u_xlat3.xyz);
    u_xlat16_8.x = u_xlat16_8.x + u_xlat16_8.x;
    u_xlat16_8.xyz = u_xlat3.xyz * (-u_xlat16_8.xxx) + u_xlat7.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, (-u_xlat7.xyz));
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_4.x = (-u_xlat0.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_4.x = u_xlat16_4.x * u_xlat16_4.x;
    u_xlat16_4.x = u_xlat16_4.x * u_xlat16_4.x;
    u_xlat0.x = (-_Glossiness) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_11.x = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_11.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_11.x;
    u_xlat16_11.x = u_xlat16_11.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_5 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, u_xlat16_8.xyz, u_xlat16_11.x);
    u_xlat16_8.x = u_xlat16_5.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_8.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_8.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_8.x = u_xlat16_8.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_8.xyz = u_xlat16_5.xyz * u_xlat16_8.xxx;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xxx * u_xlat16_8.xyz;
    u_xlat7.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat7.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x + 1.5;
    u_xlat16_22 = (-u_xlat16_22) * 0.280000001 + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_22);
    u_xlat16_22 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_11.x = (-u_xlat16_22) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_11.x = u_xlat16_11.x + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat16_11.x = clamp(u_xlat16_11.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat5.xyz = u_xlat16_5.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_6.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_11.xyz = u_xlat16_11.xxx + (-u_xlat16_6.xyz);
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xxx * u_xlat16_11.xyz + u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat14 = dot(u_xlat2.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat14 = max(u_xlat14, 0.00100000005);
    u_xlat14 = inversesqrt(u_xlat14);
    u_xlat2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat14) * u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat14 = dot(_WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat14 = clamp(u_xlat14, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat21 = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat21 = clamp(u_xlat21, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat2.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat2.x = clamp(u_xlat2.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat21 = u_xlat21 * u_xlat21;
    u_xlat14 = max(u_xlat14, 0.319999993);
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat14;
    u_xlat14 = u_xlat7.x * u_xlat7.x + -1.0;
    u_xlat14 = u_xlat21 * u_xlat14 + 1.00001001;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat14 * u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat7.x / u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x + -9.99999975e-05;
    u_xlat0.x = max(u_xlat0.x, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = min(u_xlat0.x, 100.0);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * u_xlat0.xxx;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat5.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_22) + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * u_xlat2.xxx + u_xlat16_1.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

#endif

#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x) layout(location = x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(binding = x, std140)

uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAg;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAb;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBg;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBb;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHC;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
vec4 u_xlat0;
bool u_xlatb0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
vec3 u_xlat5;
float u_xlat18;
float u_xlat19;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat0.x = (-u_xlat16_2.x) + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat0.w = u_xlat0.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.w = clamp(u_xlat0.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    u_xlat19 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat19 = inversesqrt(u_xlat19);
    u_xlat0.xyz = vec3(u_xlat19) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1 = u_xlat0;
    u_xlat1.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat1.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat1.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat18 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat18 = inversesqrt(u_xlat18);
    u_xlat1.xyz = vec3(u_xlat18) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat1.y * u_xlat1.y;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat1.x * u_xlat1.x + (-u_xlat16_2.x);
    u_xlat16_3 = u_xlat1.yzzx * u_xlat1.xyzz;
    u_xlat16_4.x = dot(unity_SHBr, u_xlat16_3);
    u_xlat16_4.y = dot(unity_SHBg, u_xlat16_3);
    u_xlat16_4.z = dot(unity_SHBb, u_xlat16_3);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = unity_SHC.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat1.w = 1.0;
    u_xlat16_3.x = dot(unity_SHAr, u_xlat1);
    u_xlat16_3.y = dot(unity_SHAg, u_xlat1);
    u_xlat16_3.z = dot(unity_SHAb, u_xlat1);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = max(u_xlat16_2.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    u_xlat5.xyz = log2(u_xlat16_2.xyz);
    u_xlat5.xyz = u_xlat5.xyz * vec3(0.416666657, 0.416666657, 0.416666657);
    u_xlat5.xyz = exp2(u_xlat5.xyz);
    u_xlat5.xyz = u_xlat5.xyz * vec3(1.05499995, 1.05499995, 1.05499995) + vec3(-0.0549999997, -0.0549999997, -0.0549999997);
    u_xlat5.xyz = max(u_xlat5.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    vs_TEXCOORD2.xyz = u_xlat5.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD2.w = 0.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x + u_xlat16_2.x;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw = u_xlat1.xyz * (-u_xlat16_2.xxx) + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.x = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, (-u_xlat0.xyz));
    u_xlat16_2.x = clamp(u_xlat16_2.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-u_xlat16_2.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * u_xlat16_2.x;
    vs_TEXCOORD5.w = u_xlat16_2.x * u_xlat16_2.x;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.x = 0.0;
    return;
}

precision highp float;
precision highp int;

uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform highp sampler2D unity_NHxRoughness;
UNITY_LOCATION(3) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
vec3 u_xlat4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
mediump float u_xlat16_7;
mediump float u_xlat16_23;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.xz = (-vec2(_Glossiness)) + vec2(1.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_1.x = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_1.x;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat16_1.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_1 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, u_xlat16_1.x);
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_1.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_2.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx;
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_23 = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_23 = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_23;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_23) * u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_23) * vs_TEXCOORD2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_4.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat4.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_5.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = (-u_xlat16_5.xyz) + vs_TEXCOORD1.www;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD5.www * u_xlat16_6.xyz + u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat16_23 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_23) * u_xlat4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_3.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz + u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_23 = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_23 = u_xlat16_23 * u_xlat16_23;
    u_xlat16_7 = u_xlat16_23 * u_xlat16_23;
    u_xlat0.y = u_xlat16_7;
    u_xlat4.x = texture(unity_NHxRoughness, u_xlat0.yz).x;
    u_xlat4.x = u_xlat4.x * 16.0;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = u_xlat4.xxx * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * u_xlat16_3.xyz + u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

precision highp float;
precision highp int;
#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1

uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAg;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAb;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec4 u_xlat0;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
vec3 u_xlat3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
float u_xlat8;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_9;
float u_xlat15;
mediump float u_xlat16_16;
float u_xlat21;
mediump float u_xlat16_21;
float u_xlat22;
mediump float u_xlat16_25;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.w = 1.0;
    u_xlat1.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz);
    u_xlat1.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat1.x);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat1.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.x = dot(unity_SHAr, u_xlat0);
    u_xlat16_2.y = dot(unity_SHAg, u_xlat0);
    u_xlat16_2.z = dot(unity_SHAb, u_xlat0);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz + vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = max(u_xlat16_2.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    u_xlat1.xyz = log2(u_xlat16_2.xyz);
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz * vec3(0.416666657, 0.416666657, 0.416666657);
    u_xlat1.xyz = exp2(u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz * vec3(1.05499995, 1.05499995, 1.05499995) + vec3(-0.0549999997, -0.0549999997, -0.0549999997);
    u_xlat1.xyz = max(u_xlat1.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    u_xlat16_21 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_21 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_2.x;
    u_xlat16_9.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat3.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_1.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_4.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_25 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = u_xlat3.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_25);
    u_xlat16_25 = (-u_xlat16_25) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_25 = u_xlat16_25 + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat16_25 = clamp(u_xlat16_25, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = (-u_xlat16_4.xyz) + vec3(u_xlat16_25);
    u_xlat16_9.xyz = u_xlat16_9.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat21 = dot(vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz);
    u_xlat21 = inversesqrt(u_xlat21);
    u_xlat1.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz) * vec3(u_xlat21) + _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz;
    u_xlat3.xyz = vec3(u_xlat21) * vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz;
    u_xlat21 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat21 = max(u_xlat21, 0.00100000005);
    u_xlat21 = inversesqrt(u_xlat21);
    u_xlat1.xyz = vec3(u_xlat21) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat21 = dot(_WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat21 = clamp(u_xlat21, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.x = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat1.x = clamp(u_xlat1.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.x = u_xlat1.x * u_xlat1.x;
    u_xlat21 = max(u_xlat21, 0.319999993);
    u_xlat8 = (-_Glossiness) + 1.0;
    u_xlat15 = u_xlat8 * u_xlat8 + 1.5;
    u_xlat21 = u_xlat21 * u_xlat15;
    u_xlat15 = u_xlat8 * u_xlat8;
    u_xlat22 = u_xlat15 * u_xlat15 + -1.0;
    u_xlat1.x = u_xlat1.x * u_xlat22 + 1.00001001;
    u_xlat21 = u_xlat21 * u_xlat1.x;
    u_xlat21 = u_xlat15 / u_xlat21;
    u_xlat16_25 = u_xlat8 * u_xlat15;
    u_xlat16_25 = (-u_xlat16_25) * 0.280000001 + 1.0;
    u_xlat21 = u_xlat21 + -9.99999975e-05;
    u_xlat21 = max(u_xlat21, 0.0);
    u_xlat21 = min(u_xlat21, 100.0);
    u_xlat1.xzw = vec3(u_xlat21) * u_xlat16_4.xyz + u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xzw = u_xlat1.xzw * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat21 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat21 = clamp(u_xlat21, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.xzw = u_xlat1.xzw * vec3(u_xlat21) + u_xlat16_9.xyz;
    u_xlat16_9.x = (-u_xlat8) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_9.x = u_xlat8 * u_xlat16_9.x;
    u_xlat16_9.x = u_xlat16_9.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_16 = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_16 = u_xlat16_16 + u_xlat16_16;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * (-vec3(u_xlat16_16)) + u_xlat3.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, (-u_xlat3.xyz));
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_16 = (-u_xlat0.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_16 = u_xlat16_16 * u_xlat16_16;
    u_xlat16_16 = u_xlat16_16 * u_xlat16_16;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_16) * u_xlat16_6.xyz + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_0 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, u_xlat16_5.xyz, u_xlat16_9.x);
    u_xlat16_9.x = u_xlat16_0.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_9.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_9.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_9.x = u_xlat16_9.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_9.xyz = u_xlat16_0.xyz * u_xlat16_9.xxx;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xxx * u_xlat16_9.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_25);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat16_4.xyz + u_xlat1.xzw;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[16];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
vec4 u_xlat0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
bool u_xlatb1;
vec4 u_xlat2;
mediump float u_xlat16_3;
float u_xlat12;
float u_xlat14;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3] * in_POSITION0.wwww + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat2 = u_xlat1.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat2 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat1.xxxx + u_xlat2;
    u_xlat2 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat1.zzzz + u_xlat2;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat1.wwww + u_xlat2;
    u_xlatb1 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat1.xy = (bool(u_xlatb1)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat1.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat16_3 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat1.x = (-u_xlat16_3) + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat1.w = u_xlat1.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat1.w = clamp(u_xlat1.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat2.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = u_xlat2.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    u_xlat14 = dot(u_xlat2.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat14 = inversesqrt(u_xlat14);
    u_xlat1.xyz = vec3(u_xlat14) * u_xlat2.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1 = u_xlat1;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat2 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[1];
    u_xlat2 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat2;
    u_xlat2 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat2;
    vs_TEXCOORD3 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat2;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat0.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat0.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat12 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat12 = inversesqrt(u_xlat12);
    u_xlat0.xyz = vec3(u_xlat12) * u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_3 = u_xlat16_3 + u_xlat16_3;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw = u_xlat0.xyz * (-vec3(u_xlat16_3)) + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, (-u_xlat1.xyz));
    u_xlat16_3 = clamp(u_xlat16_3, 0.0, 1.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3 = (-u_xlat16_3) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_3 = u_xlat16_3 * u_xlat16_3;
    vs_TEXCOORD5.w = u_xlat16_3 * u_xlat16_3;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.x = 0.0;
    return;

uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	vec4 _LightShadowData;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform highp sampler2D unity_NHxRoughness;
UNITY_LOCATION(3) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
UNITY_LOCATION(4) uniform highp sampler2D _ShadowMapTexture;
UNITY_LOCATION(5) uniform highp sampler2DShadow hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
vec3 u_xlat6;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
mediump float u_xlat16_20;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.xz = (-vec2(_Glossiness)) + vec2(1.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_1.x = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_1.x;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat16_1.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_1 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, u_xlat16_1.x);
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_1.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_2.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx;
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_20 = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_20) * u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_20 = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_6.x = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat0.y = u_xlat16_6.x;
    u_xlat0.x = texture(unity_NHxRoughness, u_xlat0.yz).x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * 16.0;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat6.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_3.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    u_xlat16_20 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat6.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_20) + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    vec3 txVec0 = vec3(vs_TEXCOORD3.xy,vs_TEXCOORD3.z);
    u_xlat0.x = textureLod(hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture, txVec0, 0.0);
    u_xlat6.x = (-_LightShadowData.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat6.x + _LightShadowData.x;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = (-u_xlat16_3.xyz) + vs_TEXCOORD1.www;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD5.www * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat16_3.xyz + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;

    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat0.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD3 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat0.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat0.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat6 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat6 = inversesqrt(u_xlat6);
    vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz = vec3(u_xlat6) * u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD5 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD7 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    return;
}

float u_xlat25;
mediump float u_xlat16_26;
mediump float u_xlat16_28;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz + (-unity_ShadowFadeCenterAndType.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = sqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat8.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz) + _WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz;
    u_xlat1.x = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[0].z;
    u_xlat1.y = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[1].z;
    u_xlat1.z = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[2].z;
    u_xlat8.x = dot(u_xlat8.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = (-u_xlat8.x) + u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat0.x = unity_ShadowFadeCenterAndType.w * u_xlat0.x + u_xlat8.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * _LightShadowData.z + _LightShadowData.w;
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat8.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD8.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[1].xyz;
    u_xlat8.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[0].xyz * vs_TEXCOORD8.xxx + u_xlat8.xyz;
    u_xlat8.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[2].xyz * vs_TEXCOORD8.zzz + u_xlat8.xyz;
    u_xlat8.xyz = u_xlat8.xyz + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[3].xyz;
    vec3 txVec0 = vec3(u_xlat8.xy,u_xlat8.z);
    u_xlat8.x = textureLod(hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture, txVec0, 0.0);
    u_xlat16 = (-_LightShadowData.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat8.x = u_xlat8.x * u_xlat16 + _LightShadowData.x;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-u_xlat8.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_2.x + u_xlat8.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat8.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz) * u_xlat0.xxx + _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat8.xyz, u_xlat8.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = max(u_xlat0.x, 0.00100000005);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat8.xyz;
    u_xlat24 = dot(_WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat24 = clamp(u_xlat24, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat24 = max(u_xlat24, 0.319999993);
    u_xlat25 = (-_Glossiness) + 1.0;
    u_xlat3.x = u_xlat25 * u_xlat25 + 1.5;
    u_xlat24 = u_xlat24 * u_xlat3.x;
    u_xlat3.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz);
    u_xlat3.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat3.x);
    u_xlat3.xyz = u_xlat3.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat8.x = u_xlat25 * u_xlat25;
    u_xlat16 = u_xlat8.x * u_xlat8.x + -1.0;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16 + 1.00001001;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat24;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat8.x / u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat16_26 = u_xlat25 * u_xlat8.x;
    u_xlat16_26 = (-u_xlat16_26) * 0.280000001 + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x + -9.99999975e-05;
    u_xlat0.x = max(u_xlat0.x, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = min(u_xlat0.x, 100.0);
    u_xlat16_8.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_8.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat8.xyz = u_xlat16_8.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_4.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat5.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_28 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat8.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_28) + u_xlat5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_28 = (-u_xlat16_28) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_28 = u_xlat16_28 + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat16_28 = clamp(u_xlat16_28, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = (-u_xlat16_4.xyz) + vec3(u_xlat16_28);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-u_xlat25) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat25 * u_xlat16_2.x;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_10 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat3.xyz);
    u_xlat16_10 = u_xlat16_10 + u_xlat16_10;
    u_xlat16_7.xyz = u_xlat3.xyz * (-vec3(u_xlat16_10)) + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat24 = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, (-u_xlat1.xyz));
    u_xlat24 = clamp(u_xlat24, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat1.x = clamp(u_xlat1.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_10 = (-u_xlat24) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_10 = u_xlat16_10 * u_xlat16_10;
    u_xlat16_10 = u_xlat16_10 * u_xlat16_10;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_10) * u_xlat16_6.xyz + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, u_xlat16_7.xyz, u_xlat16_2.x);
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_3.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_2.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_3.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx;
    u_xlat16_24 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_28 = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_28 = u_xlat16_24 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_28;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_28);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_26);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * u_xlat1.xxx + u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

#ifdef VERTEX
#version 300 es

#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1

uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAg;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAb;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBg;

uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHC;

uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
vec4 u_xlat0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
bool u_xlatb1;
vec4 u_xlat2;
vec4 u_xlat3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
vec3 u_xlat6;
float u_xlat22;
float u_xlat23;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3] * in_POSITION0.wwww + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat2 = u_xlat1.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat2 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat1.xxxx + u_xlat2;
    u_xlat2 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat1.zzzz + u_xlat2;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat1.wwww + u_xlat2;
    u_xlatb1 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat1.xy = (bool(u_xlatb1)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat1.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat16_3.x = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat1.x = (-u_xlat16_3.x) + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat1.w = u_xlat1.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat1.w = clamp(u_xlat1.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat2.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = u_xlat2.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    u_xlat23 = dot(u_xlat2.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat23 = inversesqrt(u_xlat23);
    u_xlat1.xyz = vec3(u_xlat23) * u_xlat2.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1 = u_xlat1;
    u_xlat2.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat2.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat2.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat22 = dot(u_xlat2.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat22 = inversesqrt(u_xlat22);
    u_xlat2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat22) * u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.x = u_xlat2.y * u_xlat2.y;
    u_xlat16_3.x = u_xlat2.x * u_xlat2.x + (-u_xlat16_3.x);
    u_xlat16_4 = u_xlat2.yzzx * u_xlat2.xyzz;
    u_xlat16_5.x = dot(unity_SHBr, u_xlat16_4);
    u_xlat16_5.y = dot(unity_SHBg, u_xlat16_4);
    u_xlat16_5.z = dot(unity_SHBb, u_xlat16_4);
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = unity_SHC.xyz * u_xlat16_3.xxx + u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat2.w = 1.0;
    u_xlat16_4.x = dot(unity_SHAr, u_xlat2);
    u_xlat16_4.y = dot(unity_SHAg, u_xlat2);
    u_xlat16_4.z = dot(unity_SHAb, u_xlat2);
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = u_xlat16_3.xyz + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = max(u_xlat16_3.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    u_xlat6.xyz = log2(u_xlat16_3.xyz);
    u_xlat6.xyz = u_xlat6.xyz * vec3(0.416666657, 0.416666657, 0.416666657);
    u_xlat6.xyz = exp2(u_xlat6.xyz);
    u_xlat6.xyz = u_xlat6.xyz * vec3(1.05499995, 1.05499995, 1.05499995) + vec3(-0.0549999997, -0.0549999997, -0.0549999997);
    u_xlat6.xyz = max(u_xlat6.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    vs_TEXCOORD2.xyz = u_xlat6.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD2.w = 0.0;
    u_xlat3 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[1];
    u_xlat3 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat3;
    u_xlat3 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat3;
    vs_TEXCOORD3 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat3;
    u_xlat16_4.x = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat16_4.x = u_xlat16_4.x + u_xlat16_4.x;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw = u_xlat2.xyz * (-u_xlat16_4.xxx) + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.x = dot(u_xlat2.xyz, (-u_xlat1.xyz));
    u_xlat16_4.x = clamp(u_xlat16_4.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz = u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.x = (-u_xlat16_4.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_4.x = u_xlat16_4.x * u_xlat16_4.x;
    vs_TEXCOORD5.w = u_xlat16_4.x * u_xlat16_4.x;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.x = 0.0;
    return;
}

precision highp float;

UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform highp sampler2D unity_NHxRoughness;

UNITY_LOCATION(4) uniform highp sampler2D _ShadowMapTexture;
UNITY_LOCATION(5) uniform highp sampler2DShadow hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_1;
vec3 u_xlat2;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
float u_xlat7;
mediump float u_xlat16_7;
mediump float u_xlat16_22;
void main()
{
    vec3 txVec0 = vec3(vs_TEXCOORD3.xy,vs_TEXCOORD3.z);
    u_xlat0.x = textureLod(hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture, txVec0, 0.0);
    u_xlat7 = (-_LightShadowData.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat7 + _LightShadowData.x;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_1.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xz = (-vec2(_Glossiness)) + vec2(1.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_22 = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_22;
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat16_22 * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_2 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, u_xlat16_22);
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat16_2.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_22 = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_22 + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat16_22 * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_22);
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_22 = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_22;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_22) * u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_22) * vs_TEXCOORD2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_5.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = (-u_xlat16_5.xyz) + vs_TEXCOORD1.www;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD5.www * u_xlat16_6.xyz + u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = u_xlat16_3.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat16_22 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_22) * u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    u_xlat16_22 = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat16_22 * u_xlat16_22;
    u_xlat16_7 = u_xlat16_22 * u_xlat16_22;
    u_xlat0.y = u_xlat16_7;
    u_xlat0.x = texture(unity_NHxRoughness, u_xlat0.yz).x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * 16.0;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * u_xlat16_1.xyz + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBg;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHBb;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHC;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD7;
out highp vec3 vs_TEXCOORD8;
vec4 u_xlat0;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_0;
bool u_xlatb0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
mediump float u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
float u_xlat12;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat0.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD3 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat0.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat0.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat12 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat12 = inversesqrt(u_xlat12);
    u_xlat0.xyz = vec3(u_xlat12) * u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.w = 0.0;
    u_xlat16_2 = u_xlat0.y * u_xlat0.y;
    u_xlat16_2 = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x + (-u_xlat16_2);
    u_xlat16_0 = u_xlat0.yzzx * u_xlat0.xyzz;
    u_xlat16_3.x = dot(unity_SHBr, u_xlat16_0);
    u_xlat16_3.y = dot(unity_SHBg, u_xlat16_0);
    u_xlat16_3.z = dot(unity_SHBb, u_xlat16_0);
    vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz = unity_SHC.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_2) + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD5.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD7 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    return;
}

#ifdef FRAGMENT
#version 300 es
#ifdef GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod
#extension GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod : enable
#endif

precision highp float;
precision highp int;
#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform
#endif
#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x) layout(location = x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(binding = x, std140)
#else
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(std140)
#endif
uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAr;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAg;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SHAb;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[16];
uniform 	vec4 _LightShadowData;
uniform 	vec4 unity_ShadowFadeCenterAndType;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[4];
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
UNITY_LOCATION(3) uniform highp sampler2D _ShadowMapTexture;
UNITY_LOCATION(4) uniform highp sampler2DShadow hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
in highp vec3 vs_TEXCOORD8;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
vec3 u_xlat1;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
vec4 u_xlat3;
vec3 u_xlat4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_7;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_8;
vec3 u_xlat9;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_9;
mediump float u_xlat16_11;
float u_xlat18;
mediump float u_xlat16_20;
float u_xlat27;
mediump float u_xlat16_27;
float u_xlat28;
mediump float u_xlat16_29;
mediump float u_xlat16_32;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz + (-unity_ShadowFadeCenterAndType.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = sqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat9.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz) + _WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz;
    u_xlat1.x = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[0].z;
    u_xlat1.y = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[1].z;
    u_xlat1.z = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixV[2].z;
    u_xlat9.x = dot(u_xlat9.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = (-u_xlat9.x) + u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat0.x = unity_ShadowFadeCenterAndType.w * u_xlat0.x + u_xlat9.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * _LightShadowData.z + _LightShadowData.w;
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat9.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD8.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[1].xyz;
    u_xlat9.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[0].xyz * vs_TEXCOORD8.xxx + u_xlat9.xyz;
    u_xlat9.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[2].xyz * vs_TEXCOORD8.zzz + u_xlat9.xyz;
    u_xlat9.xyz = u_xlat9.xyz + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToShadow[3].xyz;
    vec3 txVec0 = vec3(u_xlat9.xy,u_xlat9.z);
    u_xlat9.x = textureLod(hlslcc_zcmp_ShadowMapTexture, txVec0, 0.0);
    u_xlat18 = (-_LightShadowData.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat9.x = u_xlat9.x * u_xlat18 + _LightShadowData.x;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-u_xlat9.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_2.x + u_xlat9.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat9.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz) * u_xlat0.xxx + _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat9.xyz, u_xlat9.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = max(u_xlat0.x, 0.00100000005);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat9.xyz;
    u_xlat27 = dot(_WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat27 = clamp(u_xlat27, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat27 = max(u_xlat27, 0.319999993);
    u_xlat28 = (-_Glossiness) + 1.0;
    u_xlat3.x = u_xlat28 * u_xlat28 + 1.5;
    u_xlat27 = u_xlat27 * u_xlat3.x;
    u_xlat3.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz);
    u_xlat3.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat3.x);
    u_xlat3.xyz = u_xlat3.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat9.x = u_xlat28 * u_xlat28;
    u_xlat18 = u_xlat9.x * u_xlat9.x + -1.0;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat18 + 1.00001001;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat27;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat9.x / u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat16_29 = u_xlat28 * u_xlat9.x;
    u_xlat16_29 = (-u_xlat16_29) * 0.280000001 + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x + -9.99999975e-05;
    u_xlat0.x = max(u_xlat0.x, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = min(u_xlat0.x, 100.0);
    u_xlat16_9.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat4.xyz = u_xlat16_9.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_9.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_5.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_32 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = u_xlat4.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_32);
    u_xlat16_32 = (-u_xlat16_32) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_32 = u_xlat16_32 + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat16_32 = clamp(u_xlat16_32, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_7.xyz = (-u_xlat16_5.xyz) + vec3(u_xlat16_32);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat3.w = 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = dot(unity_SHAr, u_xlat3);
    u_xlat16_2.y = dot(unity_SHAg, u_xlat3);
    u_xlat16_2.z = dot(unity_SHAb, u_xlat3);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz + vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = max(u_xlat16_2.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    u_xlat4.xyz = log2(u_xlat16_2.xyz);
    u_xlat4.xyz = u_xlat4.xyz * vec3(0.416666657, 0.416666657, 0.416666657);
    u_xlat4.xyz = exp2(u_xlat4.xyz);
    u_xlat4.xyz = u_xlat4.xyz * vec3(1.05499995, 1.05499995, 1.05499995) + vec3(-0.0549999997, -0.0549999997, -0.0549999997);
    u_xlat4.xyz = max(u_xlat4.xyz, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    u_xlat16_27 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_2.x = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_27 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_2.x;
    u_xlat16_8.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xxx * u_xlat4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * u_xlat16_8.xyz;
    u_xlat27 = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat27 = clamp(u_xlat27, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * vec3(u_xlat27) + u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat16_11 = (-u_xlat28) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_11 = u_xlat28 * u_xlat16_11;
    u_xlat16_11 = u_xlat16_11 * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_20 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat3.xyz);
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_20 + u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = u_xlat3.xyz * (-vec3(u_xlat16_20)) + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat27 = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, (-u_xlat1.xyz));
    u_xlat27 = clamp(u_xlat27, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_20 = (-u_xlat27) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_20) * u_xlat16_7.xyz + u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_1 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, u_xlat16_6.xyz, u_xlat16_11);
    u_xlat16_11 = u_xlat16_1.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_11 = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_11 + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_11 = u_xlat16_11 * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_11);
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xxx * u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_29);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat0.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;

out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;

bool u_xlatb0;

mediump float u_xlat16_2;
float u_xlat9;
float u_xlat10;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat16_2 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat0.x = (-u_xlat16_2) + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat0.w = u_xlat0.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat0.w = clamp(u_xlat0.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat1.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat1.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    u_xlat10 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat10 = inversesqrt(u_xlat10);
    u_xlat0.xyz = vec3(u_xlat10) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1 = u_xlat0;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat1.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat1.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat1.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat9 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat9 = inversesqrt(u_xlat9);
    u_xlat1.xyz = vec3(u_xlat9) * u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat1.xyz);
    u_xlat16_2 = u_xlat16_2 + u_xlat16_2;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw = u_xlat1.xyz * (-vec3(u_xlat16_2)) + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2 = dot(u_xlat1.xyz, (-u_xlat0.xyz));
    u_xlat16_2 = clamp(u_xlat16_2, 0.0, 1.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz = u_xlat1.xyz;
    u_xlat16_2 = (-u_xlat16_2) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2 = u_xlat16_2 * u_xlat16_2;
    vs_TEXCOORD5.w = u_xlat16_2 * u_xlat16_2;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.x = 0.0;
    return;
}

precision highp float;

#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1

uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform highp sampler2D unity_NHxRoughness;
UNITY_LOCATION(3) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
in mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_1;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_2;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_5;
vec3 u_xlat6;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
mediump float u_xlat16_20;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0.xz = (-vec2(_Glossiness)) + vec2(1.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_1.x = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_1.x;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat16_1.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_1 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, u_xlat16_1.x);
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_1.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_2.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_2.x = u_xlat16_2.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * u_xlat16_2.xxx;
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_20 = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat16_20) * u_xlat16_2.xyz;
    u_xlat16_20 = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.yzw, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat16_20 = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat16_6.x = u_xlat16_20 * u_xlat16_20;
    u_xlat0.y = u_xlat16_6.x;
    u_xlat0.x = texture(unity_NHxRoughness, u_xlat0.yz).x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * 16.0;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_6.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat6.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_3.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    u_xlat16_20 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat6.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_20) + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD5.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz;
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = (-u_xlat16_3.xyz) + vs_TEXCOORD1.www;
    u_xlat16_3.xyz = vs_TEXCOORD5.www * u_xlat16_5.xyz + u_xlat16_3.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat16_2.xyz * u_xlat16_3.xyz + u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS

uniform 	vec3 _WorldSpaceCameraPos;
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[4];
uniform 	vec4 hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[4];
uniform 	vec4 _MainTex_ST;
uniform 	vec4 _DetailAlbedoMap_ST;
uniform 	mediump float _UVSec;
in highp vec4 in_POSITION0;
in mediump vec3 in_NORMAL0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec2 in_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD2;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD3;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
out mediump vec4 vs_TEXCOORD5;
out highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD7;
out highp vec3 vs_TEXCOORD8;
vec4 u_xlat0;
bool u_xlatb0;
vec4 u_xlat1;
float u_xlat6;
void main()
{
    u_xlat0 = in_POSITION0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1];
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0] * in_POSITION0.xxxx + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2] * in_POSITION0.zzzz + u_xlat0;
    u_xlat0 = u_xlat0 + hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3];
    u_xlat1 = u_xlat0.yyyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[1];
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[0] * u_xlat0.xxxx + u_xlat1;
    u_xlat1 = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[2] * u_xlat0.zzzz + u_xlat1;
    gl_Position = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_MatrixVP[3] * u_xlat0.wwww + u_xlat1;
    u_xlatb0 = _UVSec==0.0;
    u_xlat0.xy = (bool(u_xlatb0)) ? in_TEXCOORD0.xy : in_TEXCOORD1.xy;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.zw = u_xlat0.xy * _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.xy + _DetailAlbedoMap_ST.zw;
    vs_TEXCOORD0.xy = in_TEXCOORD0.xy * _MainTex_ST.xy + _MainTex_ST.zw;
    u_xlat0.xyz = in_POSITION0.yyy * hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[1].xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[0].xyz * in_POSITION0.xxx + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[2].xyz * in_POSITION0.zzz + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_ObjectToWorld[3].xyz * in_POSITION0.www + u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz + (-_WorldSpaceCameraPos.xyz);
    vs_TEXCOORD8.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD1.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD3 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[0].xyz);
    u_xlat0.y = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[1].xyz);
    u_xlat0.z = dot(in_NORMAL0.xyz, hlslcc_mtx4x4unity_WorldToObject[2].xyz);
    u_xlat6 = dot(u_xlat0.xyz, u_xlat0.xyz);
    u_xlat6 = inversesqrt(u_xlat6);
    vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz = vec3(u_xlat6) * u_xlat0.xyz;
    vs_TEXCOORD4.w = 0.0;
    vs_TEXCOORD5 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vs_TEXCOORD7 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    return;
}

#ifdef FRAGMENT

precision highp float;
precision highp int;
#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform

#else
#define UNITY_LOCATION(x)
#define UNITY_BINDING(x) layout(std140)
#endif
uniform 	mediump vec4 _WorldSpaceLightPos0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 unity_SpecCube0_HDR;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _LightColor0;
uniform 	mediump vec4 _Color;
uniform 	mediump float _Metallic;
uniform 	float _Glossiness;
uniform 	mediump float _OcclusionStrength;
UNITY_LOCATION(0) uniform mediump sampler2D _MainTex;
UNITY_LOCATION(1) uniform mediump sampler2D _OcclusionMap;
UNITY_LOCATION(2) uniform mediump samplerCube unity_SpecCube0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD0;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD1;
in highp vec4 vs_TEXCOORD4;
layout(location = 0) out mediump vec4 SV_Target0;
vec3 u_xlat0;
mediump float u_xlat16_0;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_1;
vec3 u_xlat2;
vec3 u_xlat3;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_4;
vec3 u_xlat5;
mediump vec4 u_xlat16_5;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_6;
vec3 u_xlat7;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_8;
mediump vec3 u_xlat16_11;
float u_xlat14;
float u_xlat21;
mediump float u_xlat16_22;
void main()
{
    u_xlat16_0 = texture(_OcclusionMap, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).y;
    u_xlat16_1.x = (-_OcclusionStrength) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_1.x = u_xlat16_0 * _OcclusionStrength + u_xlat16_1.x;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat7.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz;
    u_xlat2.xyz = (-vs_TEXCOORD1.xyz) * u_xlat0.xxx + _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz, vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz);
    u_xlat0.x = inversesqrt(u_xlat0.x);
    u_xlat3.xyz = u_xlat0.xxx * vs_TEXCOORD4.xyz;
    u_xlat16_8.x = dot(u_xlat7.xyz, u_xlat3.xyz);
    u_xlat16_8.x = u_xlat16_8.x + u_xlat16_8.x;
    u_xlat16_8.xyz = u_xlat3.xyz * (-u_xlat16_8.xxx) + u_xlat7.xyz;
    u_xlat0.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, (-u_xlat7.xyz));
    u_xlat0.x = clamp(u_xlat0.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_4.x = (-u_xlat0.x) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_4.x = u_xlat16_4.x * u_xlat16_4.x;
    u_xlat16_4.x = u_xlat16_4.x * u_xlat16_4.x;
    u_xlat0.x = (-_Glossiness) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_11.x = (-u_xlat0.x) * 0.699999988 + 1.70000005;
    u_xlat16_11.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat16_11.x;
    u_xlat16_11.x = u_xlat16_11.x * 6.0;
    u_xlat16_5 = textureLod(unity_SpecCube0, u_xlat16_8.xyz, u_xlat16_11.x);
    u_xlat16_8.x = u_xlat16_5.w + -1.0;
    u_xlat16_8.x = unity_SpecCube0_HDR.w * u_xlat16_8.x + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_8.x = u_xlat16_8.x * unity_SpecCube0_HDR.x;
    u_xlat16_8.xyz = u_xlat16_5.xyz * u_xlat16_8.xxx;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xxx * u_xlat16_8.xyz;
    u_xlat7.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat16_22 = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat7.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat0.x + 1.5;
    u_xlat16_22 = (-u_xlat16_22) * 0.280000001 + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_22);
    u_xlat16_22 = (-_Metallic) * 0.779083729 + 0.779083729;
    u_xlat16_11.x = (-u_xlat16_22) + 1.0;
    u_xlat16_11.x = u_xlat16_11.x + _Glossiness;
    u_xlat16_11.x = clamp(u_xlat16_11.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat16_5.xyz = texture(_MainTex, vs_TEXCOORD0.xy).xyz;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = _Color.xyz * u_xlat16_5.xyz + vec3(-0.220916301, -0.220916301, -0.220916301);
    u_xlat5.xyz = u_xlat16_5.xyz * _Color.xyz;
    u_xlat16_6.xyz = vec3(vec3(_Metallic, _Metallic, _Metallic)) * u_xlat16_6.xyz + vec3(0.220916301, 0.220916301, 0.220916301);
    u_xlat16_11.xyz = u_xlat16_11.xxx + (-u_xlat16_6.xyz);
    u_xlat16_4.xyz = u_xlat16_4.xxx * u_xlat16_11.xyz + u_xlat16_6.xyz;
    u_xlat16_1.xyz = u_xlat16_1.xyz * u_xlat16_4.xyz;
    u_xlat14 = dot(u_xlat2.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat14 = max(u_xlat14, 0.00100000005);
    u_xlat14 = inversesqrt(u_xlat14);
    u_xlat2.xyz = vec3(u_xlat14) * u_xlat2.xyz;
    u_xlat14 = dot(_WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat14 = clamp(u_xlat14, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat21 = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, u_xlat2.xyz);
    u_xlat21 = clamp(u_xlat21, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat2.x = dot(u_xlat3.xyz, _WorldSpaceLightPos0.xyz);
    u_xlat2.x = clamp(u_xlat2.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    u_xlat21 = u_xlat21 * u_xlat21;
    u_xlat14 = max(u_xlat14, 0.319999993);
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x * u_xlat14;
    u_xlat14 = u_xlat7.x * u_xlat7.x + -1.0;
    u_xlat14 = u_xlat21 * u_xlat14 + 1.00001001;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat14 * u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat7.x / u_xlat0.x;
    u_xlat0.x = u_xlat0.x + -9.99999975e-05;
    u_xlat0.x = max(u_xlat0.x, 0.0);
    u_xlat0.x = min(u_xlat0.x, 100.0);
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat16_6.xyz * u_xlat0.xxx;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat5.xyz * vec3(u_xlat16_22) + u_xlat0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * _LightColor0.xyz;
    u_xlat0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz * u_xlat2.xxx + u_xlat16_1.xyz;
    SV_Target0.xyz = u_xlat0.xyz;
    SV_Target0.w = 1.0;
    return;
}

#ifdef VERTEX
#version 300 es

#define HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS 1
#if HLSLCC_ENABLE_UNIFORM_BUFFERS
#define UNITY_UNIFORM
#else
#define UNITY_UNIFORM uniform
#endif
#define UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION 1
#if UNITY_SUPPORTS_UNIFORM_LOCATION
